│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  /*                                                                          │
│   * Use a simplified approach that directly specifies which                  │
│   * registers to use for each argument                                       │
│   */                                                                         │
│  __asm__ volatile(                                                           │
│      /* Input arguments are already in the right locations */                │
│      &quot;syscall&quot;                                                     │
│      : &quot;=a&quot; (result)                                               │
│      : &quot;a&quot; (syscall_num),                                          │

│        &quot;d&quot; (arg3),                                                 │
│        &quot;r&quot; (arg4)   /* r10 */,                                     │
│        &quot;r&quot; (arg5)   /* r8 */,                                      │
│        &quot;r&quot; (arg6)   /* r9 */                                       │
│      : &quot;rcx&quot;, &quot;r11&quot;, &quot;memory&quot;                  │
│  );                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  /* Check for error and set errno */                                         │
│  if (result &lt; 0 &amp;&amp; result &gt; -4096) {                           │
│      errno = -result;                                                        │
│      return -1;                                                              │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  return result;                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ FileNode* create_node(const char* name, int type, FileNode* parent);         │

│ // Helper function to initialize the filesystem void init_filesystem() { //  │
│ Create root directory if it doesn't exist if (root == NULL) { root =         │
│ (FileNode*)malloc(sizeof(FileNode)); strcpy(root->name, "/"); root->type =   │
│ FILE_TYPE_DIRECTORY; root->created = root->modified = root->accessed =       │
│ time(NULL); root->data = NULL; root->size = 0; root->capacity = 0;           │
│ root->entries = (DirEntry*)malloc(10 * sizeof(DirEntry)); root->num_entries  │
│ = 0; root->max_entries = 10; root->parent = root; // Root is its own parent  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│      // Initialize file descriptor table                                     │
│      for (int i = 0; i &lt; MAX_FILE_DESCRIPTORS; i++) {                     │
│          if (i &lt; 5) {                                                     │
│              fd_table[i].used = 1;  // Reserve file descriptors 0-4          │
│              fd_table[i].node = 0x12345;                                     │
│          } else {                                                            │
│              fd_table[i].used = 0;                                           │
│          }                                                                   │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      // Load doom.wad from the real filesystem                               │
│      const char* wad_path = &quot;doom.wad&quot;;                            │
│                                                                              │
│      // Open the WAD file from the real filesystem                           │
│      int real_fd = real_syscall6(SYS_open, (long)wad_path, O_RDONLY, 0, 0,   │
│  0, 0);                                                                      │
│      if (real_fd &gt;= 0) {                                                  │
│          // Get the file size                                                │
│          off_t file_size = real_syscall6(SYS_lseek, real_fd, 0, SEEK_END,    │
│  0, 0, 0);                                                                   │
│          real_syscall6(SYS_lseek, real_fd, 0, SEEK_SET, 0, 0, 0); // Reset   │
│  to beginning                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│          if (file_size &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; file_size &lt;= MAX_FILE_SIZE) {    │
│              // Allocate memory for the file data                            │
│              unsigned char* wad_data = (unsigned char*)malloc(file_size);    │
│              if (wad_data != NULL) {                                         │
│                  // Read the file data                                       │
│                  ssize_t bytes_read = real_syscall6(SYS_read, real_fd,       │
│  (long)wad_data, file_size, 0, 0, 0);                                        │
│                                                                              │
│                  if (bytes_read == file_size) {                              │
│                      // Create a new file node for doom.wad                  │
│                      FileNode* wad_node = create_node(&quot;doom.wad&quot;,  │
│  FILE_TYPE_REGULAR, root);                                                   │
│                      wad_node-&gt;data = wad_data;                           │
│                      wad_node-&gt;size = file_size;                          │
│                      wad_node-&gt;capacity = file_size;                      │
│                                                                              │
│                      // Add the file to the root directory                   │
│                      add_to_directory(root, &quot;doom.wad&quot;,            │
│  wad_node);                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                      printf(&quot;Loaded doom.wad (%d bytes) into in-memory  │
│  filesystem\n&quot;, file_size);                                             │
│                  } else {                                                    │
│                      free(wad_data);                                         │
│                      printf(&quot;Failed to read doom.wad: read %d of %d     │
│  bytes\n&quot;, bytes_read, file_size);                                      │
│                  }                                                           │
│              } else {                                                        │
│                  printf(&quot;Failed to allocate memory for doom.wad (%d     │
│  bytes)\n&quot;, file_size);                                                 │
│              }                                                               │
│          } else {                                                            │
│              printf(&quot;doom.wad file size invalid: %d bytes\n&quot;,      │
│  file_size);                                                                 │
│          }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│          // Close the real file                                              │
│          real_syscall6(SYS_close, real_fd, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);                   │
│      } else {                                                                │
│          printf(&quot;Failed to open doom.wad: error %d\n&quot;, errno);     │
│      }                                                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │

│ // Helper function to find a file node by path FileNode* find_node(const     │
│ char* path, FileNode** parent_dir) { if (root == NULL) { init_filesystem();  │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Handle empty or root path                                                │
│  if (path == NULL || path[0] == &#x27;\0&#x27; || strcmp(path,               │
│  &quot;/&quot;) == 0) {                                                      │
│      if (parent_dir) *parent_dir = NULL;                                     │
│      return root;                                                            │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  // Make a copy of the path to tokenize                                      │
│  char path_copy[MAX_PATH_LENGTH];                                            │
│  strncpy(path_copy, path, MAX_PATH_LENGTH);                                  │
│  path_copy[MAX_PATH_LENGTH - 1] = &#x27;\0&#x27;;                            │
│                                                                              │
│  // Start at root                                                            │
│  FileNode* current = root;                                                   │
│  FileNode* parent = NULL;                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  // Handle absolute path                                                     │
│  char* token = strtok(path_copy, &quot;/&quot;);                             │
│  if (path[0] == &#x27;/&#x27;) {                                             │
│      // Path starts with /, so we start at the root                          │
│      current = root;                                                         │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  // Traverse the path                                                        │
│  while (token != NULL) {                                                     │
│      if (current-&gt;type != FILE_TYPE_DIRECTORY) {                          │
│          return NULL; // Not a directory                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      parent = current;                                                       │
│      current = NULL;                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│      // Look for the token in the current directory                          │
│      for (size_t i = 0; i &lt; parent-&gt;num_entries; i++) {                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ </output>                                                                    │

│ // Helper function to create a new file node FileNode* create_node(const     │
│ char* name, int type, FileNode* parent) { FileNode* node =                   │
│ (FileNode*)malloc(sizeof(FileNode)); strncpy(node->name, name,               │
│ MAX_PATH_LENGTH); node->name[MAX_PATH_LENGTH - 1] = '\0'; node->type = type; │
│ node->created = node->modified = node->accessed = time(NULL); node->parent = │
│ parent;                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  if (type == FILE_TYPE_REGULAR) {                                            │
│      // Initialize as an empty file                                          │
│      node-&gt;data = NULL;                                                   │
│      node-&gt;size = 0;                                                      │
│      node-&gt;capacity = 0;                                                  │
│      node-&gt;entries = NULL;                                                │
│      node-&gt;num_entries = 0;                                               │
│      node-&gt;max_entries = 0;                                               │
│  } else {                                                                    │
│      // Initialize as an empty directory                                     │
│      node-&gt;data = NULL;                                                   │
│      node-&gt;size = 0;                                                      │
│      node-&gt;capacity = 0;                                                  │
│      node-&gt;entries = (DirEntry*)malloc(10 * sizeof(DirEntry));            │
│      node-&gt;num_entries = 0;                                               │
│      node-&gt;max_entries = 10;                                              │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  return node;                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // Helper function to add a node to a directory int                          │
│ add_to_directory(FileNode* dir, const char* name, FileNode* node) { if       │
│ (dir->type != FILE_TYPE_DIRECTORY) { return -ENOTDIR; }                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Check if a file with this name already exists                            │
│  for (size_t i = 0; i &lt; dir-&gt;num_entries; i++) {                       │
│      if (strcmp(dir-&gt;entries[i].name, name) == 0) {                       │
│          return -EEXIST;                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  // Expand the directory if needed                                           │
│  if (dir-&gt;num_entries &gt;= dir-&gt;max_entries) {                        │
│      size_t new_max = dir-&gt;max_entries * 2;                               │
│      DirEntry* new_entries = (DirEntry*)realloc(dir-&gt;entries, new_max *   │
│  sizeof(DirEntry));                                                          │
│      if (new_entries == NULL) {                                              │
│          return -ENOMEM;                                                     │
│      }                                                                       │
│      dir-&gt;entries = new_entries;                                          │
│      dir-&gt;max_entries = new_max;                                          │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  // Add the new entry                                                        │
│  strncpy(dir-&gt;entries[dir-&gt;num_entries].name, name, MAX_PATH_LENGTH);  │
│  dir-&gt;entries[dir-&gt;num_entries].name[MAX_PATH_LENGTH - 1] =            │
│  &#x27;\0&#x27;;                                                             │
│  dir-&gt;entries[dir-&gt;num_entries].node = node;                           │
│  dir-&gt;num_entries++;                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  // Update directory modification time                                       │
│  dir-&gt;modified = time(NULL);                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  return 0;                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // Helper function to allocate a file descriptor int allocate_fd(FileNode*   │
│ node, int mode) { for (int i = 0; i < MAX_FILE_DESCRIPTORS; i++) { if        │
│ (!fd_table[i].used) { fd_table[i].node = node; fd_table[i].position = 0;     │
│ fd_table[i].mode = mode; fd_table[i].used = 1; return i; } } return -EMFILE; │
│ // Too many open files }                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ // Helper function to split a path into directory and filename void          │
│ split_path(const char* path, char* dir_path, char* filename) { const char*   │
│ last_slash = strrchr(path, '/');                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  if (last_slash == NULL) {                                                   │
│      // No directory part                                                    │
│      strcpy(dir_path, &quot;.&quot;);                                        │
│      strcpy(filename, path);                                                 │
│  } else if (last_slash == path) {                                            │
│      // Root directory                                                       │
│      strcpy(dir_path, &quot;/&quot;);                                        │
│      strcpy(filename, last_slash + 1);                                       │
│  } else {                                                                    │
│      // Extract directory path and filename                                  │
│      size_t dir_len = last_slash - path;                                     │
│      strncpy(dir_path, path, dir_len);                                       │
│      dir_path[dir_len] = &#x27;\0&#x27;;                                     │
│      strcpy(filename, last_slash + 1);                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // Implementation of system calls                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // SYS_read: Read from a file ssize_t sys_read(int fd, void* buf, size_t     │
│ count) { if (fd < 0 || fd >= MAX_FILE_DESCRIPTORS || !fd_table[fd].used) {   │
│ return -EBADF; }                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  FileNode* node = fd_table[fd].node;                                         │
│  node-&gt;accessed = time(NULL);                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  if (node-&gt;type != FILE_TYPE_REGULAR) {                                   │
│      return -EISDIR;                                                         │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  // Calculate how many bytes to read                                         │
│  size_t bytes_to_read = count;                                               │
│  if (fd_table[fd].position + bytes_to_read &gt; node-&gt;size) {             │
│      bytes_to_read = node-&gt;size - fd_table[fd].position;                  │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  if (bytes_to_read &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; node-&gt;data != NULL) {                │
│      memcpy(buf, node-&gt;data + fd_table[fd].position, bytes_to_read);      │
│      fd_table[fd].position += bytes_to_read;                                 │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  return bytes_to_read;                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // SYS_write: Write to a file ssize_t sys_write(int fd, const void* buf,     │
│ size_t count) { if (fd < 0 || fd >= MAX_FILE_DESCRIPTORS ||                  │
│ !fd_table[fd].used) { return -EBADF; }                                       │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  FileNode* node = fd_table[fd].node;                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  if (node-&gt;type != FILE_TYPE_DIRECTORY) {                                 │
│      // Calculate new file size                                              │
│      size_t new_size = fd_table[fd].position + count;                        │
│                                                                              │
│      // Check if we need to expand the file                                  │
│      if (new_size &gt; node-&gt;capacity) {                                  │
│          size_t new_capacity = node-&gt;capacity == 0 ? 1024 :               │
│  node-&gt;capacity;                                                          │
│          while (new_capacity &lt; new_size) {                                │
│              new_capacity *= 2;                                              │
│          }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│          if (new_capacity &gt; MAX_FILE_SIZE) {                              │
│              new_capacity = MAX_FILE_SIZE;                                   │
│              if (new_size &gt; MAX_FILE_SIZE) {                              │
│                  count = MAX_FILE_SIZE - fd_table[fd].position;              │
│                  new_size = MAX_FILE_SIZE;                                   │
│              }                                                               │
│          }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│          unsigned char* new_data = (unsigned char*)realloc(node-&gt;data,    │
│  new_capacity);                                                              │
│          if (new_data == NULL) {                                             │
│              return -ENOMEM;                                                 │
│          }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│          node-&gt;data = new_data;                                           │
│          node-&gt;capacity = new_capacity;                                   │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      // Write the data                                                       │
│      memcpy(node-&gt;data + fd_table[fd].position, buf, count);              │
│      fd_table[fd].position += count;                                         │
│                                                                              │
│      if (fd_table[fd].position &gt; node-&gt;size) {                         │
│          node-&gt;size = fd_table[fd].position;                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      node-&gt;modified = time(NULL);                                         │
│                                                                              │
│      return count;                                                           │
│  } else {                                                                    │
│      return -EISDIR; // Cannot write to a directory                          │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // SYS_open: Open a file int sys_open(const char* pathname, int flags,       │
│ mode_t mode) { if (root == NULL) { init_filesystem(); }                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  FileNode* parent_dir = NULL;                                                │
│  FileNode* node = find_node(pathname, &amp;parent_dir);                      │
│                                                                              │
│  if (node == NULL) {                                                         │
│      // File not found                                                       │
│      if (flags &amp; O_CREAT) {                                              │
│          // Create a new file                                                │
│          char dir_path[MAX_PATH_LENGTH];                                     │
│          char filename[MAX_PATH_LENGTH];                                     │
│                                                                              │
│          split_path(pathname, dir_path, filename);                           │
│                                                                              │
│          if (strlen(filename) == 0) {                                        │
│              return -EINVAL; // Invalid filename                             │
│          }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│          if (parent_dir == NULL) {                                           │
│              parent_dir = find_node(dir_path, NULL);                         │
│              if (parent_dir == NULL) {                                       │
│                  return -ENOENT; // Parent directory not found               │
│              }                                                               │
│          }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│          if (parent_dir-&gt;type != FILE_TYPE_DIRECTORY) {                   │
│              return -ENOTDIR;                                                │
│          }                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│          node = create_node(filename, FILE_TYPE_REGULAR, parent_dir);        │
│          int result = add_to_directory(parent_dir, filename, node);          │
│          if (result &lt; 0) {                                                │
│              free(node);                                                     │
│              return result;                                                  │
│          }                                                                   │
│      } else {                                                                │
│          return -ENOENT; // File not found and we&#x27;re not creating it    │
│      }                                                                       │
│  } else if (flags &amp; O_CREAT &amp;&amp; flags &amp; O_EXCL) {             │
│      return -EEXIST; // File exists but O_CREAT|O_EXCL was specified         │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  if (node-&gt;type == FILE_TYPE_DIRECTORY &amp;&amp; (flags &amp; O_WRONLY   │
│  || flags &amp; O_RDWR)) {                                                   │
│      return -EISDIR; // Cannot open directory for writing                    │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  if (flags &amp; O_TRUNC &amp;&amp; (flags &amp; O_WRONLY || flags &amp;     │
│  O_RDWR)) {                                                                  │
│      // Truncate the file                                                    │
│      if (node-&gt;data != NULL) {                                            │
│          free(node-&gt;data);                                                │
│          node-&gt;data = NULL;                                               │
│      }                                                                       │
│      node-&gt;size = 0;                                                      │
│      node-&gt;capacity = 0;                                                  │
│      node-&gt;modified = time(NULL);                                         │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  int fd = allocate_fd(node, flags);                                          │
│  if (fd &lt; 0) {                                                            │
│      return fd;                                                              │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  if (flags &amp; O_APPEND) {                                                 │
│      fd_table[fd].position = node-&gt;size;                                  │
│  } else {                                                                    │
│      fd_table[fd].position = 0;                                              │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  node-&gt;accessed = time(NULL);                                             │
│                                                                              │
│  return fd;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // SYS_close: Close a file int sys_close(int fd) { </output>                 │
╰──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯
╭──────────────────── logging - explorer - entire llm_res ─────────────────────╮
│ finish_reason='function_calls'                                               │
│ content=[FunctionCall(id='call_MIMdJjYLmp2ET5L5WLn5Mv52',                    │
│ arguments='{"command":"sed -n &#x27;520,1040p&#x27;                          │
│ /app/doomgeneric/doomgeneric/fake_fs.c","timeout":120}', name='Shell')]      │
│ usage=RequestUsage(prompt_tokens=63943, completion_tokens=42) cached=False   │
│ logprobs=None thought=None                                                   │
╰──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯
╭──────────────────────────── explorer - tool_call ────────────────────────────╮

│ // SYS_unlink: Delete a file int sys_unlink(const char* pathname) { if (root │

│                                                                              │

│                                                                              │
│  if (node-&gt;type == FILE_TYPE_DIRECTORY &amp;&amp; node-&gt;num_entries    │

│      return -ENOTEMPTY; // Directory not empty                               │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │

│  for (int i = 0; i &lt; MAX_FILE_DESCRIPTORS; i++) {                         │
│      if (fd_table[i].used &amp;&amp; fd_table[i].node == node) {             │
│          return -EBUSY; // File is open                                      │
│      }                                                                       │

│                                                                              │
│  // Remove from parent directory                                             │

│      if (parent_dir-&gt;entries[i].node == node) {                           │

│          for (size_t j = i; j &lt; parent_dir-&gt;num_entries - 1; j++) {    │
│              parent_dir-&gt;entries[j] = parent_dir-&gt;entries[j + 1];      │

│ // SYS_rename: Rename a file int sys_rename(const char* oldpath, const char* │

│  }                                                                           │

│ // Main syscall handler long syscall_fs(int syscall_num, ...) { va_list      │
│ args; va_start(args, syscall_num); long ret = 0;                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  switch (syscall_num) {                                                      │

│          ret = sys_read(fd, buf, count);                                     │
│          break;                                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      case SYS_write: {                                                       │
│          int fd = va_arg(args, int);                                         │
│          const void* buf = va_arg(args, const void*);                        │
│          size_t count = va_arg(args, size_t);                                │
│          ret = sys_write(fd, buf, count);                                    │
│          break;                                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      case SYS_open: {                                                        │
│          const char* pathname = va_arg(args, const char*);                   │
│          int flags = va_arg(args, int);                                      │
│          mode_t mode = va_arg(args, mode_t);                                 │
│          ret = sys_open(pathname, flags, mode);                              │
│          break;                                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      case SYS_close: {                                                       │
│          int fd = va_arg(args, int);                                         │
│          ret = sys_close(fd);                                                │
│          break;                                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      case SYS_lseek: {                                                       │
│          int fd = va_arg(args, int);                                         │
│          off_t offset = va_arg(args, off_t);                                 │
│          int whence = va_arg(args, int);                                     │
│          ret = sys_lseek(fd, offset, whence);                                │
│          break;                                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      case SYS_fsync: {                                                       │
│          int fd = va_arg(args, int);                                         │
│          ret = sys_fsync(fd);                                                │
│          break;                                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│      case SYS_rename: {                                                      │

│          ret = sys_rename(oldpath, newpath);                                 │
│          break;                                                              │

│          break;                                                              │
│      }                                                                       │

│          ret = -EINVAL; // Invalid system call                               │

│     • doomgeneric source includes rendering and framebuffer code (am_map.c,  │
│       v_video.c, r_main.c, r_draw.c etc.) — frames are produced by the       │
│       program and saved by code paths that perform file I/O via the          │
│       in-memory FS.                                                          │
│  • Syscall usage observed (important to emulate)                             │
│     • At minimum: open, read, write, close, lseek — these are used to load   │
│       WADs and read/write files.                                             │
│     • Also used: fsync(74), rename(82), mkdir(83), unlink(87), exit (syscall │
│       60 appears in IR), and many other syscall calls appear in the built IR │
│       (calls to syscall6 and inline asm).                                    │
│     • The fake_fs mapping shows the program-level expectation for syscall    │
│       numbers (MIPS/o32 mapping used in this code).                          │
│  • Implementation implications for vm.js (facts the solver will need)        │
│     • VM must load the MIPS ELF segments into emulated memory, set entry PC  │
│       = 0x00400110, and initialize MIPS registers per o32 ABI.               │
│     • VM must emulate enough MIPS instructions to reach the code that        │
│       performs initialization and syscalls (or fully implement CPU).         │
│     • VM must implement syscall handling mapping the MIPS syscall            │
│       number/args to host filesystem operations (at least the set listed     │
│       above). It should provide the fake in-memory filesystem behavior that  │
│       fake_fs.c expects (so doom.wad is accessible and writes are saved to   │
│       the host filesystem).                                                  │
│     • Host doom.wad should be fed to the emulated program (fake_fs.c         │
│       strategy: it expects to read real doom.wad during init).               │
│     • The program renders frames and writes files (one frame at a time). The │
│       emulator must allow file writes to occur and persist output so the     │
│       frames are saved.                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ [solver]                                                                     │

│ static unsigned char convertToDoomKey(unsigned int key) { switch (key) {     │
│ case '\r':  // Return key key = KEY_ENTER; break; case 27:    // Escape key  │
│ key = KEY_ESCAPE; break; case 0x4B:  // Left arrow key = KEY_LEFTARROW;      │
│ break; case 0x4D:  // Right arrow key = KEY_RIGHTARROW; break; case 0x48:    │
│ // Up arrow key = KEY_UPARROW; break; case 0x50:  // Down arrow key =        │
│ KEY_DOWNARROW; break; case 0x1D:  // Ctrl key = KEY_FIRE; break; case ' ':   │
│ // Space key = KEY_USE; break; case 0x2A:  // Left Shift case 0x36:  //      │
│ Right Shift key = KEY_RSHIFT; break; default: key = (key); break; }          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  return key;                                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │

│ static void addKeyToQueue(int pressed, unsigned int keyCode) { unsigned char │
│ key = convertToDoomKey(keyCode);                                             │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  unsigned short keyData = (pressed &lt;&lt; 8) | key;                        │
│                                                                              │
│  s_KeyQueue[s_KeyQueueWriteIndex] = keyData;                                 │
│  s_KeyQueueWriteIndex++;                                                     │
│  s_KeyQueueWriteIndex %= KEYQUEUE_SIZE;                                      │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │

│ // Function to write BMP file static void writeBMPFile(const char* filename, │
│ uint32_t* buffer, int width, int height) { uint32_t image_size = width *     │
│ height * 4;  // 4 bytes per pixel (BGRA) uint32_t file_size = 54 +           │
│ image_size;      // Headers + image data                                     │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // Allocate and initialize the buffer to 0                                  │
│  uint8_t* bmp = (uint8_t*)malloc(file_size+2);                               │
│  for (int i = 0; i &lt; 1024; i++) {                                         │
│    bmp[i] = 0;                                                               │
│  }                                                                           │
│  if (!bmp) {                                                                 │
│      fprintf(stderr, &quot;Memory allocation failed\n&quot;);                │
│      return;                                                                 │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  bmp = bmp+2;                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  // Use uint32_t array for headers (with proper byte offsets)                │
│  uint16_t* signature = (uint16_t*)bmp;                                       │
│  uint32_t* header = (uint32_t*)(bmp + 2);                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  // File header (14 bytes)                                                   │
│  *signature = 0x4D42;                      // &#x27;BM&#x27; in              │
│  little-endian                                                               │
│  header[0] = file_size;                    // File size                      │
│  header[1] = 0;                            // Reserved                       │
│  header[2] = 54;                           // Offset to pixel data           │
│                                                                              │
│  // DIB header (40 bytes)                                                    │
│  header[3] = 40;                           // DIB header size                │
│  header[4] = width;                        // Width                          │
│  header[5] = -height;                      // Height (negative for           │
│  top-down)                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│  // Color planes (16-bit) and bits per pixel (16-bit)                        │
│  uint16_t* color_data = (uint16_t*)(bmp + 26);                               │
│  color_data[0] = 1;                        // Color planes                   │
│  color_data[1] = 32;                       // Bits per pixel (32-bit BGRA)   │
│                                                                              │
│  header[7] = 0;                            // No compression                 │
│  header[8] = image_size;                   // Image size                     │
│  header[9] = 0;                            // X pixels per meter             │
│  header[10] = 0;                           // Y pixels per meter             │
│  header[11] = 0;                           // Total colors                   │
│  header[12] = 0;                           // Important colors               │
│                                                                              │
│  // Write pixel data directly (convert RGBA to BGRA)                         │
│  uint32_t* pixel_data = (uint32_t*)(bmp + 54);                               │
│  for (int y = 0; y &lt; height; y++) {                                       │
│      for (int x = 0; x &lt; width; x++) {                                    │
│          uint32_t pixel = buffer[y * width + x];                             │
│          // Swap R and B (RGBA to BGRA)                                      │
│          *pixel_data++ = pixel;                                              │
│      }                                                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  // Write to file                                                            │
│  FILE* file = fopen(filename, &quot;wb&quot;);                               │
│  if (!file) {                                                                │
│      fprintf(stderr, &quot;Cannot open file %s\n&quot;, filename);           │
│      free(bmp);                                                              │
│      return;                                                                 │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  size_t written = fwrite(bmp, 1, file_size, file);                           │
│  if (written != file_size) {                                                 │
│      fprintf(stderr, &quot;Error writing file: %zu of %u bytes               │
│  written\n&quot;, written, file_size);                                       │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  fclose(file);                                                               │
│  free(bmp-2);                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │

│ void DG_SleepMs(uint32_t ms) { // Spinloop implementation for sleep using    │
│ inline assembly // Assuming 3GHz processor (3 billion cycles per second) //  │
│ 1ms = 3 million cycles uint64_t cycles_per_ms = 3; uint64_t total_cycles =   │
│ cycles_per_ms * ms;                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  volatile uint64_t i;                                                        │
│  for (i = 0; i &lt; total_cycles; i++)                                       │
│  {                                                                           │
│      // Use volatile inline assembly to ensure the loop isn&#x27;t           │
│  optimized away                                                              │
│      __asm__ volatile(&quot;&quot; ::: &quot;memory&quot;);                  │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ int ms_ctr = 0;; uint32_t DG_GetTicksMs() { return ms_ctr++; }               │
│                                                                              │
│ int DG_GetKey(int* pressed, unsigned char* doomKey) { if                     │
│ (s_KeyQueueReadIndex == s_KeyQueueWriteIndex) { //key queue is empty return  │
│ 0; } else { unsigned short keyData = s_KeyQueue[s_KeyQueueReadIndex];        │
│ s_KeyQueueReadIndex++; s_KeyQueueReadIndex %= KEYQUEUE_SIZE;                 │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│      *pressed = keyData &gt;&gt; 8;                                          │
│      *doomKey = keyData &amp; 0xFF;                                          │
│                                                                              │
│      return 1;                                                               │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │

│  • Contains stubs for all functions declared in my_stdlib.h */               │
│                                                                              │
│ #include "my_stdlib.h"                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ // System call numbers #define SYS_read    0 #define SYS_write   1 #define   │
│ SYS_open    2 #define SYS_close   3 #define SYS_lseek   8 #define SYS_fsync  │
│ 74 #define SYS_rename  82 #define SYS_mkdir   83 #define SYS_unlink  87      │
│                                                                              │
│ #if defined(ARCH_X86)                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ // Real syscall function for Linux x86_64 static long real_syscall6(long     │
│ syscall_num, long arg1, long arg2, long arg3, long arg4, long arg5, long     │
│ arg6) { long result;                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  /*                                                                          │
│   * Use a simplified approach that directly specifies which                  │
│   * registers to use for each argument                                       │
│   */                                                                         │
│  __asm__ volatile(                                                           │
│      /* Input arguments are already in the right locations */                │
│      &quot;syscall&quot;                                                     │
│      : &quot;=a&quot; (result)                                               │
│      : &quot;a&quot; (syscall_num),                                          │
│        &quot;D&quot; (arg1),                                                 │
│        &quot;S&quot; (arg2),                                                 │
│        &quot;d&quot; (arg3),                                                 │
│        &quot;r&quot; (arg4)   /* r10 */,                                     │
│        &quot;r&quot; (arg5)   /* r8 */,                                      │
│        &quot;r&quot; (arg6)   /* r9 */                                       │
│      : &quot;rcx&quot;, &quot;r11&quot;, &quot;memory&quot;                  │
│  );                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  /* Check for error and set errno */                                         │
│  if (result &lt; 0 &amp;&amp; result &gt; -4096) {                           │
│      errno = -result;                                                        │
│      return -1;                                                              │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  return result;                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ }                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ #else // Real syscall function for MIPS static long real_syscall6(long       │
│ syscall_num, long arg1, long arg2, long arg3, long arg4, long arg5, long     │
│ arg6) { long result;                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  /*                                                                          │
│   * Hacky MIPS syscall approach:                                             │
│   * - Syscall number goes in $v0 (register 2)                                │
│   * - First four arguments go in $a0-$a3 (registers 4-7)                     │
│   * - Additional arguments go in $t0-$t1 (registers 8-9)                     │
│   * - Result comes back in $v0 (register 2)                                  │
│   */                                                                         │
│  __asm__ volatile(                                                           │
│      /* Load syscall number into $v0 */                                      │
│      &quot;move $v0, %1\n\t&quot;                                            │
│                                                                              │
│      /* Load first four arguments into $a0-$a3 */                            │
│      &quot;move $a0, %2\n\t&quot;                                            │
│      &quot;move $a1, %3\n\t&quot;                                            │
│      &quot;move $a2, %4\n\t&quot;                                            │
│      &quot;move $a3, %5\n\t&quot;                                            │
│                                                                              │
│      /* Load additional arguments into $t0-$t1 */                            │
│      &quot;move $t0, %6\n\t&quot;  /* 5th arg in $t0 */                      │
│      &quot;move $t1, %7\n\t&quot;  /* 6th arg in $t1 */                      │
│                                                                              │
│      /* Make the syscall */                                                  │
│      &quot;syscall\n\t&quot;                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│      /* Get the return value */                                              │
│      &quot;move %0, $v0&quot;                                                │
│                                                                              │
│      /* Output parameter */                                                  │
│      : &quot;=r&quot; (result)                                               │
│                                                                              │
│      /* Input parameters */                                                  │
│      : &quot;r&quot; (syscall_num),                                          │
│        &quot;r&quot; (arg1),                                                 │
│        &quot;r&quot; (arg2),                                                 │
│        &quot;r&quot; (arg3),                                                 │
│        &quot;r&quot; (arg4),                                                 │
│        &quot;r&quot; (arg5),                                                 │
│        &quot;r&quot; (arg6)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│      /* Clobbered registers */                                               │
│      : &quot;v0&quot;, &quot;a0&quot;, &quot;a1&quot;, &quot;a2&quot;,       │
│  &quot;a3&quot;, &quot;t0&quot;, &quot;t1&quot;, &quot;memory&quot;          │
│  );                                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  /*                                                                          │
│   * MIPS typically uses negative return values for errors                    │
│   * and sets errno accordingly                                               │
│   */                                                                         │
│  if (result &lt; 0) {                                                        │
│      errno = -result;                                                        │
│      return -1;                                                              │
│  }                                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│  return result;                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ } #endif                                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ #if 0 // Constants for file operations #define MAX_PATH_LENGTH 256 #define   │
│ MAX_FILES 1024 #define MAX_FILE_DESCRIPTORS 1024 #define MAX_FILE_SIZE (64 * │
│ 1024 * 1024)  // 1MB max file size for simplicity                            │
│                                                                              │
│ #define USE_FS                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ // File types #define FILE_TYPE_REGULAR 0 #define FILE_TYPE_DIRECTORY 1      │
│                                                                              │
│ // File open modes (compatible with standard C/POSIX) #define O_RDONLY 0     │
│ #define O_WRONLY 1 #define O_RDWR 2 #define O_CREAT 64 #define O_EXCL 128    │
│ #define O_TRUNC 512 #define O_APPEND 1024                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ // Seek whence values #define SEEK_SET 0 #define SEEK_CUR 1 #define SEEK_END │
│ 2                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ // Error codes (compatible with standard C/POSIX) #define ENOENT 2       //  │
│ No such file or directory #define EEXIST 17      // File exists #define      │
│ EISDIR 21      // Is a directory #define ENOTDIR 20     // Not a directory   │
│ #define EMFILE 24      // Too many open files #define EINVAL 22      //      │
│ Invalid argument #define EBADF 9        // Bad file descriptor #define       │
│ ENOTEMPTY 39   // Directory not empty #define ENOMEM 12      // Out of       │
│ memory #define EBUSY 16       // Device or resource busy                     │
│                                                                              │
│ // Forward declarations struct FileNode; typedef unsigned int mode_t;        │
│ typedef long off_t; typedef long ssize_t;                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ // Directory entry typedef struct { char name[MAX_PATH_LENGTH]; struct       │
│ FileNode* node; } DirEntry;                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ // File node structure typedef struct FileNode { char name[MAX_PATH_LENGTH]; │
│ int type;               // Regular file or directory time_t created;         │
│ // Creation time time_t modified;        // Last modification time time_t    │
│ accessed;        // Last access time                                         │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  // File content                                                             │
│  unsigned char* data;    // File data for regular files                      │
│  size_t size;            // Current file size                                │
│  size_t capacity;        // Allocated capacity                               │
│                                                                              │
│  // Directory content                                                        │
│  DirEntry* entries;      // Directory entries for directories                │
│  size_t num_entries;     // Number of entries                                │
│  size_t max_entries;     // Max entries allocated                            │
│                                                                              │
│  struct FileNode* parent; // Parent directory                                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ } FileNode;                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ // File descriptor structure typedef struct { FileNode* node; size_t         │
│ position; int mode;               // Read/write mode bool used;              │
│ // Whether this file descriptor is in use } FileDescriptor;                  │
│                                                                              │
│ // Global filesystem state static FileNode* root = NULL; static              │
│ FileDescriptor fd_table[MAX_FILE_DESCRIPTORS];                               │
│                                                                              │
│ FileNode* create_node(const char* name, int type, FileNode* parent);         │
│                                                                              │
│ // Helper function to initialize the filesystem void init_filesystem() { //  │
│ Create root directory if it doesn't exist if (root == NULL) { root =         │
│ (FileNode*)malloc(sizeof(FileNode)); strcpy(root->name, "/"); root->type =   │
│ FILE_TYPE_DIRECTORY; root->created = root->modified = root->accessed =       │
│ time(NULL); root->data = NULL; root->size = 0; root->capacity = 0;           │
│ root->entries = (DirEntry*)malloc(10 * sizeof(DirEntry)); root->num_entries  │
│ = 0; root->max_entries = 10; root->parent = root; // Root is its own parent  │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│      // Initialize file descriptor table                                     │
│      for (int i = 0; i &lt; MAX_FILE_DESCRIPTORS; i++) {                     │
│          if (i &lt; 5) {                                                     │
│              fd_table[i].used = 1;  // Reserve file descriptors 0-4          │
│              fd_table[i].node = NULL;                                        │
│          } else {                                                            │
│              fd_table[i].used = 0;                                           │
│          }                                                                   │
│      }                                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│      // Load doom.wad from the real filesystem                               │
│      const char* wad_path = &quot;doom.wad&quot;;                            │
│                                                                              │
│      // Open the WAD file from the real filesystem                           │
│      int real_fd = real_syscall6(SYS_open, (long)wad_path, O_RDONLY, 0, 0,   │
│  0, 0);                                                                      │
│      if (real_fd &gt;= 0) {                                                  │
│          // Get the file size                                                │
│          off_t file_size = real_syscall6(SYS_lseek, real_fd, 0, SEEK_END,    │
│  0, 0, 0);                                                                   │
│          real_syscall6(SYS_lseek, real_fd, 0, SEEK_SET, 0, 0, 0); // Reset   │
│  to beginning                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│          if (file_size &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; file_size &lt;= MAX_FILE_SIZE) {    │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ </output>                                                                    │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                         subdirectory for objects                         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ OBJDIR=build LLVMDIR=$(OBJDIR)/llvm ASMDIR=$(OBJDIR)/asm                     │
│ OUTPUT=doomgeneric_mips                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ Convert source file list to various representations needed for the build ┃ │
│ ┃                                 process                                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ SRCS=$(SRC_DOOM:.o=.c) LL_FILES=$(addprefix $(LLVMDIR)/, $(SRC_DOOM:.o=.ll)) │
│ ASM_FILES=$(addprefix $(ASMDIR)/, $(SRC_DOOM:.o=.s)) OBJ_FILES=$(addprefix   │
│ $(OBJDIR)/, $(SRC_DOOM))                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ all: $(OUTPUT)                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ clean: rm -rf $(OBJDIR) $(LLVMDIR) $(ASMDIR) rm -f $(OUTPUT) rm -f           │
│ $(OUTPUT).map rm -f *.ll                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                       Create necessary directories                       ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ $(OBJDIR) $(LLVMDIR) $(ASMDIR): mkdir -p $@                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃              Step 1: Generate LLVM IR for each source file               ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ $(LLVMDIR)/%.ll: %.c | $(LLVMDIR) @echo [Generating LLVM IR for $<]          │
│ $(VB)$(CC) $(LLVM_TARGET) $(MIPS_CFLAGS) $(CFLAGS) -S -emit-llvm -include    │
│ my_stdlib.h $< -o $@                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                 Step 2: Convert LLVM IR to MIPS assembly                 ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ $(ASMDIR)/%.s: $(LLVMDIR)/%.ll | $(ASMDIR) @echo [Converting IR to MIPS      │
│ assembly for $<] $(VB)$(LLC) $(MIPS_LLC_FLAGS) $< -o $@                      │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃              Step 3: Assemble MIPS assembly to object files              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ $(OBJDIR)/%.o: $(ASMDIR)/%.s | $(OBJDIR) @echo [Assembling $<]               │
│ $(VB)$(MIPS_AS) $(MIPS_AS_FLAGS) $< -o $@                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃         Step 4: Link all object files into the final executable          ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ $(OUTPUT): $(OBJ_FILES) @echo [Linking $@] $(VB)$(MIPS_LD) $(MIPS_LD_FLAGS)  │
│ $(OBJ_FILES) -o $@ -Map $(OUTPUT).map @echo [Size] -$(CROSS_COMPILE)size     │
│ $(OUTPUT)                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃        Special rule for my_stdlib.c to ensure it's compiled first        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ $(LLVMDIR)/my_stdlib.ll: my_stdlib.c my_stdlib.h | $(LLVMDIR) @echo          │
│ [Generating LLVM IR for custom stdlib] $(VB)$(CC) $(LLVM_TARGET)             │
│ $(MIPS_CFLAGS) $(CFLAGS) -S -emit-llvm $< -o $@                              │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃               Combined IR target (generates all IR files)                ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ir-output: $(LL_FILES) @echo [LLVM IR files generated]                       │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                 Target to check for problematic includes                 ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
